Retries are permitted only before the first complete payload for the exact same source object/request, preserving source family, applicable period, requested URL, request parameters, and provider/domain exactly -- no alternate URL/provider/mirror/month/suffix/parameter change. Retryable transport classes are exactly `NETWORK_TIMEOUT`, `CONNECTION_RESET`, `TEMPORARY_DNS_FAILURE`, `HTTP_408`, `HTTP_425`, `HTTP_429`, `HTTP_500`, `HTTP_502`, `HTTP_503`, `HTTP_504`; no other condition is retryable, and nonretryable conditions (off-domain/untrusted redirect, response-host mismatch, parser/schema failure, semantic/data-quality failure, missing/ambiguous evidence, governance/implementation failure) stop immediately with no attempts consumed.

- New GPT prefreeze methodology decision recorded (docs-only; not yet implemented in code): `V9_006_STAGE_A_SEMANTIC_VALIDATION_METHODOLOGY.md` binds original V9_006 HIGH_2's (full semantic reconstruction/validation) exact methodology for `SECURITY_TYPE`, `CANONICAL_IDENTITY`, `LISTING_TRANSITIONS`/`DELISTING_TRANSITIONS`/`MARKET_TRANSITIONS`, `EFFECTIVE_DATE`, and `RECONSTRUCTION`. `canonical_security_identity = canonical_code`: the exact 4-character canonical code, UTF-8/ASCII, no prefix/suffix/company-name/market/date; only unambiguous spreadsheet-artifact stripping (whitespace, integral `.0`) and ASCII uppercasing are permitted; JPX's 2024+ alphanumeric codes are explicitly in scope (not digits-only), and a 5-character code using a reserved security-type character is never the canonical ordinary-common identity. `AMBIGUOUS_REUSED_SECURITY_CODE` fails Stage A when the same code represents more than one disjoint genuine listing episode, with no company-name/future-data/outcome disambiguation; a mere continuously-listed name change is not a new identity. Point-in-time `listed_state`/market-state/`security_type_state` reconstruction uses exact half-open effective-date semantics (`listed_state=false` strictly before a listing date, `true` on/after; the mirror for delisting); missing/ambiguous dates fail `effective_date_pass`, and conflicting same-code/same-date transitions without a mechanically unique official ordering fail (no manual ordering). `security_type_state` is exactly `ELIGIBLE_DOMESTIC_ORDINARY_COMMON` / `EXPLICITLY_INELIGIBLE` / `UNKNOWN`; `ELIGIBLE` requires official proof of both domestic/TSE listing category and ordinary-common-stock code form; ETF/ETP/REIT/foreign/preferred/class/special/new-share products are `EXPLICITLY_INELIGIBLE`; any `UNKNOWN` needed for a reconstructed identity/date fails `security_type_pass`; eligibility must never be inferred from price availability, TOPIX membership, survival, name text, today's state, or outcomes. `listing_transition_pass`/`delisting_transition_pass` must be computed from parsed official transition events (not merely F2 family coverage, and F2/F3 conflicts fail); `market_transition_pass` must independently prove every encountered transition's exact effective date and must not merely proxy `listing_transition_pass`. `canonical_identity_pass` requires valid-grammar codes, no same-date duplicates, and no ambiguous/reused-code condition for every reconstructed candidate identity. `deterministic_reconstruction_pass` adds a reverse/forward consistency check (replay backward through the chronology then forward using the same canonical events; the recovered terminal state must byte-match the canonical terminal state, no reconciliation on mismatch) in addition to the existing byte-identical-repeated-run requirement.

- `src/v9_005_stage_a_jpx_probe.py` is rewired to consume this engine: `reconstruct_security_state`/`reconstruction_is_deterministic` now take `terminal_identities`/`events` and delegate to the semantics engine, replacing the placeholder that always reported `reconstructed_identity_count=0`. `compute_stage_a_evidence` now takes a `semantic_result` mapping and feeds its seven semantic booleans directly into the `FREE_JPX_METADATA_PROBE_PASS` conjunction; the old `_family_fully_covered`-based proxies (`listing_transition_pass==F2 coverage`, `delisting_transition_pass==F3 coverage`, `market_transition_pass==listing_transition_pass`, `canonical_identity_pass==terminal_snapshot_locked AND security_type_pass`, `effective_date_pass` as a coverage conjunction) are all removed; `terminal_snapshot_pass`/`trading_calendar_pass` are unchanged (not semantic-evidence items). `run_stage_a()` calls `compute_semantic_validation_result(terminal_identities={}, events=())` explicitly -- production has no acquired F2-F7 semantic evidence and this call site remains unreachable today (blocked by `verify_acquisition_implementation_ready()` first) -- so the engine's own fail-closed empty-input default governs, never a hardcoded or caller-supplied arbitrary PASS boolean.

- Finding 1: `compute_semantic_validation_result` never validated a `terminal_identities` entry's shape or field types, and normalized-code collisions (e.g. `"1301"` vs. `" 1301 "`, or `"130a"` vs. `"130A"`) were resolved by silent dict-assignment overwrite (`identities[code] = state`) rather than being rejected -- a caller could smuggle a non-`TerminalIdentityState` value, a non-bool `listed_state` (including a truthy int like `1`), an empty/non-string `market_state`, or an out-of-enum `security_type_state` straight into semantic reconstruction, and a genuine code collision would silently resolve to whichever entry was inserted last.
- Remediation (`src/v9_005_stage_a_semantics.py` only -- `src/v9_005_stage_a_jpx_probe.py` not touched): a new `_validate_terminal_identity_state` helper independently checks `listed_state` (must be an actual `bool` -- `isinstance(1, bool)` is `False`, so a truthy int is correctly rejected), `market_state` (non-empty `str`, no market enumeration invented), and `security_type_state` (exactly one of `VALID_SECURITY_TYPE_STATES`); a non-`TerminalIdentityState` value fails all three. Each field's invalidity fails closed exactly the specified evidence items: invalid `listed_state` -> `listing_transition_pass`/`delisting_transition_pass`/`canonical_identity_pass`/`deterministic_reconstruction_pass`; invalid `market_state` -> `market_transition_pass`/`canonical_identity_pass`/`deterministic_reconstruction_pass`; invalid `security_type_state` -> `security_type_pass`/`canonical_identity_pass`/`deterministic_reconstruction_pass`. Canonical-code collision detection now groups raw `terminal_identities` keys by normalized `canonical_code` first; any code with more than one distinct raw key is `DUPLICATE_CANONICAL_IDENTITY` -- neither entry is used, and `canonical_identity_pass`/`deterministic_reconstruction_pass` both fail, even when the colliding states are byte-identical. The exact canonical-code grammar and all other previously reviewed methodology (events, conflict/corroboration dedup, reused-code detection, reverse/forward reconstruction, UNKNOWN-while-listed) are unchanged; orphan-event-identity rejection and the two-run determinism gate were explicitly not touched.
- `tests/test_v9_005_stage_a_semantics.py` grows from 45 to 57 offline tests, adding coverage for an out-of-enum `security_type_state`, an empty/non-string `market_state`, `listed_state` as int `1`/`0`, a non-`TerminalIdentityState` mapping value, whitespace- and case-normalized code collisions, proof that a collision is never silently overwritten (neither raw key's state survives into `canonical_state`/`reconstructed_identity_count`), proof that byte-identical colliding states still fail, and a regression check that a normal single-key valid terminal identity behaves unchanged. All 45 pre-existing tests continue to pass; zero real network (structurally verified -- the module has no network-capable imports).

Since every field now resolves to a closed enum or bounded integer, no allowed key -- top-level or nested -- can carry arbitrary free-form text. The reviewed allowed top-level key set is unchanged (no key added or removed); every rejection still raises through the same `_blocked(..., raw_bytes_read_for_integrity=True, child_content_inspected=True)` path, preserving the MEDIUM-1 phase-provenance contract. `_default_structural_inspector`'s output already satisfies the strict schema unchanged, so default production behavior is preserved exactly. - Finding: the prior MEDIUM-3 remediation's five closed-set membership checks (`status` against `OUTCOMES`; `container_format`; `open_parse_status`; a dimension's `visibility`; a dimension's `object_type`) each used a bare `value not in some_frozenset` test. `in` on a `frozenset` hashes its operand first, so an unhashable malformed value (a `list` or `dict`, e.g. `status=[]`, `container_format={}`) raises a raw `TypeError` from inside the membership test itself, before the intended `ProbeBlocked` path is reached -- reproduced directly: `_safe_structural_evidence({"status": []})` raised `TypeError: unhashable type: 'list'`. Because this can occur after the Phase-C structural-inspection boundary has already been crossed, an escaping `TypeError` would be caught only by the CLI's generic non-`ProbeBlocked` fallback, incorrectly reporting `raw_bytes_read_for_integrity="unknown"`/`child_content_inspected=false` for a failure that in fact occurred after the CHILD bytes were read and structural inspection had begun.
- Remediation in `src/v9_006_f6_offline_child_structural_probe.py` adds `_is_allowed_enum_str(value, allowed) -> bool` (`isinstance(value, str) and value in allowed`), making every closed-set membership check total for arbitrary Python objects, applied to all five enum checks (`status`, `container_format`, `open_parse_status`, `visibility`, `object_type`). No enum member, allowed key, or evidence category was added, removed, or broadened -- the exact schema from the prior MEDIUM-3 remediation is preserved unchanged. As defense in depth, `run_offline_child_structural_probe`'s Phase-C boundary now also wraps the `_safe_structural_evidence(...)` call inside the same `try`/`except ProbeBlocked`/`except Exception` block already used for the `structural_inspector(...)` call (previously called unprotected, after that block); any unexpected exception during safe-evidence validation -- not only the fixed unhashable-value case -- is now translated to `ProbeBlocked(IMPLEMENTATION_FAILURE, raw_bytes_read_for_integrity=True, child_content_inspected=True)` rather than escaping. An already-correct `ProbeBlocked` from `_safe_structural_evidence` is still re-raised the same way as before, preserving its outcome and true/true fields.

- Frozen methodology: (2) before any `DATE` cell value is read, the reviewed structural inspector's evidence must be recomputed, canonicalized (`json.dumps(evidence, sort_keys=True, separators=(",", ":")).encode("utf-8")`), SHA-256-hashed, and compared to the frozen `4332d0b27a1e35256abef4c0e240b2c576c20122a264374ea0c5da3729beacce` -- any mismatch is `IMPLEMENTATION_FAILURE` with no `DATE` value read; (3) the only date-bearing columns are the preregistered 1-based ordinals `4` and `6` on sheet `1`, never selected or searched for; (4) the exact same reviewed `xlrd.open_workbook(file_contents=..., formatting_info=True, on_demand=False, ragged_rows=False)` call reopens the verified bytes; (5) for each column independently, every row is inspected via `cell_type` first, `cell_value` is read only when `cell_type == xlrd.XL_CELL_DATE`, and `xlrd.xldate_as_tuple(serial, book.datemode)[0]` extracts only the integer year -- never the serial, month, day, or time; the encountered `DATE` count per column must equal that column's own hash-verified structural-evidence count, or `IMPLEMENTATION_FAILURE`; (6) a deterministic per-column year histogram (`{year, count}`, ascending) is built from parsed `DATE` cells only; (7) the two columns' complete histograms must be exactly identical or the result is `F6_YEAR_COVERAGE_AMBIGUOUS` with no covered-year set accepted and no union/intersection/majority-vote/nearest-match/manual resolution permitted, returned to GPT for a methodology decision; (8) when identical, `covered_years`/`covered_required_years`/`missing_required_years`/`all_required_years_covered` are derived against the fixed `2017..2025` required-years set from `V9_006_STAGE_A_F6_GLOBAL_COVERAGE_METHODOLOGY.md`, a required year proven only by appearing in both columns' histograms.

- GPT-5.6 Sol exact-SHA review of `8ae9b2b8d194aa8e54a3351ce5e0b443676bdb54` is recorded as `PASS` with `CRITICAL=0`, `HIGH=0`, `MEDIUM=0`, and `LOW=1`. The sole LOW finding is `DECISION_LOG_NEIGHBOR_SENTENCE_AMBIGUOUS_SAFE_TO_DEFER`; it is safe to defer and blocks nothing.

It must not be described as if `2020-10-01` was classified inactive by TOPIX activity evidence. The already-recorded `V9_013_LEFT_DIFF_COUNT=0` and `V9_013_MISSING_EXPECTED_EXCEPTION_COUNT=1` are consistent with that reading. This clarification is additive; it changes no historical record, no frozen V9_012 or V9_013 result, and no evidential capacity.

V9_014 is not a retry, repair, or continuation of V9_012 or V9_013; those remain `V9_012_RESULT=FAIL_TERMINAL` and `V9_013_RESULT=DIAGNOSTIC_COMPLETE` with `V9_013_DIAGNOSTIC_CLASS=RELATION_OR_SENTINEL_FAILURE`.
- Frozen coverage is exactly `2017-01-01..2026-01-31`, requiring exactly `109` consecutive SOURCE_B monthly objects `2017-01..2026-01`, consistent with the recorded `V9_010_CALENDAR_REQUIRED_SOURCE_MONTH_COUNT=109`. SOURCE_A is the existing official J-Quants `/markets/calendar` scheduled TSE business-day superset with the same semantic role as V9_012; its frozen scheduled-open interpretation (`HolDiv in {"1","2"}`, strict date/coverage/uniqueness/full-coverage rules, `hol_div` absent not null) is inherited unchanged.
- SOURCE_B is the official JPX/TSE Monthly Statistics Report archive, Report 2 "Stock Trading Volume & Value", daily table "Trading Volume & Value (Daily)", with semantic role `ACTUAL_TSE_REGULAR_AUCTION_ACTIVITY_DATE_EVIDENCE`, resolved through the already-frozen V9_006 official monthly-archive traversal discipline without hardcoded archive numbering. TOPIX/index OHLC is prohibited as activity evidence, and total stock volume alone is prohibited because `2020-10-01` had no auction executions while ToSTNeT executions existed.
- Per date and per required domestic-stock market segment, the implementation must parse total Trading Volume, parse "of which ToSTNeT" Trading Volume, parse the declared unit explicitly, and normalize exactly to integer shares before arithmetic, with no floating-point conversion anywhere on the path. The frozen checks are `total >= 0`, `ToSTNeT >= 0`, `ToSTNeT <= total`, and `auction_volume = total - ToSTNeT`. A date is `ACTUAL_AUCTION_ACTIVE` iff the exact sum of `auction_volume` over the frozen required domestic-stock segments is `> 0`, and inactive iff that exact sum is `== 0`. Blank, dash, malformed, or ambiguous cells are never silently treated as zero; each is a data-quality failure unless the frozen era schema explicitly marks that segment as nonexistent or not applicable.
- Era handling is explicit and preregistered, never inferred post hoc: legacy market-segment schema through `2022-03`; the special `2022-04` market-restructure transition with `2022-04-01` on the pre-restructure schema and `2022-04-04` onward on the post-restructure schema; post-restructure Prime / Standard / Growth schema thereafter; and explicit accounting for the official 2020 unit/layout change affecting the ToSTNeT fields, where declared units drive normalization and no default unit exists.
- The frozen validation relation, evaluated only after SOURCE_A and SOURCE_B both pass, is `EXPECTED_EXCEPTION_SET={"2020-10-01"}` with `scheduled_open_dates - actual_auction_active_dates == EXPECTED_EXCEPTION_SET` and `actual_auction_active_dates - scheduled_open_dates == empty set`. Sentinels are `2020-09-30 active=true`, `2020-10-01 active=false`, `2020-10-02 active=true`. Any mismatch is terminal `ACTUAL_TRADING_DAY_AUTHORITY_FAILURE`. No manual deletion of `2020-10-01`, hard-coded correction in the produced calendar, post-hoc override, alternate source substitution after seeing results, or retry/redraw until PASS is authorized.
- Final `trading_dates` may be materialized only in a later reviewed implementation/execution stage after all frozen validations pass. V9_014 does not resolve V9_009 HIGH_2 until design PASS, then implementation PASS, then authorized real acquisition/execution, then exact frozen validation PASS, then canonical artifact review PASS.

## 2026-09-01 — V9_014 design HIGH_1/HIGH_2 source-semantics and era-binding remediation

- GPT-5.6 Sol exact-SHA review of `05d0ed3ca5cea8e84ff386fb73c65dba575030c2` is recorded as `BLOCK` with `CRITICAL=0`, `HIGH=2`, `MEDIUM=0`. `HIGH_1=JPX_REPORTED_UNIT_QUANTIZATION_INVALIDATES_EXACT_SHARE_AND_ZERO_INACTIVITY_RULE`; `HIGH_2=SOURCE_B_ERA_AND_OBJECT_BINDING_INCOMPLETE_INCLUDING_2022_04_SPLIT`. This task remediates both findings; no V9_014 design PASS is claimed.
- HIGH_1: the prior claim that reported `thous.shs.` values can be reconstructed as exact share counts is DELETED as invalid, because JPX reports omit quantities below the indicated unit. Reported-value semantics are now intervals over exact integers: for numeric integer `q` with declared share-unit multiplier `m`, `m=1` yields `[q,q]` and `m>1` yields `[q*m, q*m+(m-1)]`. Binary floating point remains prohibited on the entire parsing, normalization, comparison, and aggregation path, and the declared unit is still parsed explicitly per object, report, table, and column with no default.
- HIGH_1 adjudication is now proof-directional. Per required segment: `DQ failure` iff `total_upper < tostnet_lower`; `DEFINITELY_AUCTION_ACTIVE` iff `total_lower > tostnet_upper`; otherwise `NOT_PROVEN`, which is never silently treated as zero, inactive, closed, or absent. A date is `PROVEN_AUCTION_ACTIVE` iff at least one required segment for its era is `DEFINITELY_AUCTION_ACTIVE`. No `PROVEN_INACTIVE` state is derivable from SOURCE_B; a date that is not proven active is unproven, not inactive.
- HIGH_1 token semantics: a numeric `0` with `m>1` is not an exact zero and takes interval `[0,m-1]`; a dash / "Nil or no value" token is not malformed and never by itself proves positive activity or exact zero, so a required segment with a dash in its total or ToSTNeT cell can never be `DEFINITELY_AUCTION_ACTIVE`; mixed or structurally impossible token combinations fail closed unless the frozen era schema explicitly marks the segment out-of-era or not applicable; a blank required in-era cell remains a data-quality failure.
- HIGH_1 relation: the frozen relation is replaced by `EXPECTED_UNPROVEN_SET={"2020-10-01"}` with `scheduled_open_dates - proven_auction_active_dates == EXPECTED_UNPROVEN_SET` and `proven_auction_active_dates - scheduled_open_dates == empty set`. The retained neighbor sentinels are `2020-09-30 PROVEN_AUCTION_ACTIVE=true` and `2020-10-02 PROVEN_AUCTION_ACTIVE=true`. `2020-10-01` deliberately has no SOURCE_B activity sentinel: SOURCE_B is required only to leave it unproven, and describing it as proven inactive by SOURCE_B is exactly the invalid inference HIGH_1 removes.
- HIGH_1 adds SOURCE_C with `provider=OFFICIAL_JPX_TSE_MARKET_NEWS` and `role=EXCEPTIONAL_FULL_DAY_AUCTION_CLOSURE_AUTHORITY`, bound to the fixed public document identity of the JPX English Market News dated `2020-10-01` titled "Treatment of Trades for Today at arrowhead", whose required semantic content is that the Auction Market had no execution that day and that ToSTNeT orders received by 08:56 had executions. `source_c_confirmed_exception_set` is derived from that document's own official date and verified content, must equal exactly `{"2020-10-01"}`, and permits no manually injected date. The frozen cross-source consistency requirement is that the unproven set equals the SOURCE_C confirmed exception set exactly.
- HIGH_1 defers the final calendar: `actual_trading_dates = scheduled_open_dates - source_c_confirmed_exception_set` may be formed only if SOURCE_B proves every other scheduled-open date `PROVEN_AUCTION_ACTIVE` and all frozen relations and sentinels pass. The subtraction is authority-driven; no date is deleted by hand, hard-coded, or injected.
- HIGH_2: required domestic-stock proof segments are frozen as 1st Section, 2nd Section, Mothers, JASDAQ Standard, and JASDAQ Growth for `ERA_PRE` through `2022-04-01`, and Prime, Standard, and Growth for `ERA_POST` from `2022-04-04`. TOKYO PRO Market is not a required SOURCE_B proof segment, contributes no proof, and its absence or dash content is not a data-quality failure.
- HIGH_2: `logical_coverage_month_count=109` is frozen as a month count, distinct from the required physical SOURCE_B object count of `110`. The 2022-04 logical month is a two-part source bundle — the official "(Reference) Status on April 1, 2022" PRE object for `2022-04-01`, and the normal Report 2 monthly POST object for `2022-04-04` onward. It must not be claimed that one physical Report 2 object covers April 1; both parts are mandatory.
- HIGH_2: the archive locator remains semantic and fail-closed — official Monthly Statistics archive root, required year, Report 2 "Stock Trading Volume & Value", required month — with the special official "(Reference) Status on April 1, 2022" branch for the PRE April object. Objects are PDF only, each must resolve to a unique same-domain object, archive numbering is never hardcoded, and no URL may be guessed or pattern-derived.
- HIGH_2 unit-era binding: Mothers ToSTNeT Trading Volume is `shs.` for `2017-01..2019-12` and `thous.shs.` from `2020-01`. The declared source unit must still be parsed and match the preregistered expectation, and an unexpected unit or layout change is a data-quality failure. For other required segment columns the mechanical preregistration rule is unit constancy within era, recorded as a mechanical closure under `AI_RESEARCH_CHECKPOINT_WORKFLOW.md` §5 that introduces no unit token values not read from the source.
- All five previously open items are closed by GPT decision and no `CHATGPT_DECISION_REQUIRED` item remains in this design. `OPEN_1` closed by the frozen per-era segment sets; `OPEN_2` closed by requiring SOURCE_A to reuse the preserved immutable V9_012 SOURCE_A chain `aee49fac48358be373ac4efbcf0568b796c68fa31177e0f34c5031352297fe45` with `page_count=1`, with no fresh SOURCE_A acquisition and a later protected read still requiring fresh human authorization; `OPEN_3` closed by the locator, PDF-only format, and 2022-04 bundle; `OPEN_4` closed by the Mothers ToSTNeT unit era and declared-unit matching rule; `OPEN_5` closed by deciding that a scheduled-open date missing entirely from required in-era date/table coverage is a data-quality failure, never inactive, never an accepted exception, and never zero.
- `V9_012_RESULT=FAIL_TERMINAL`, `V9_013_RESULT=DIAGNOSTIC_COMPLETE`, `V9_013_DIAGNOSTIC_CLASS=RELATION_OR_SENTINEL_FAILURE`, `V9_009_HIGH_2=OPEN_REQUIRES_HISTORICAL_JPX_CALENDAR_BINDING`, `V9_009_MEDIUM_1=OPEN`, `T0_STATUS=NOT_RUN`, and `future_profitability_established=false` all remain unchanged. This design-remediation-only task made no implementation, code, or test change, and performed no JPX/J-Quants/Yahoo/external-data acquisition, protected or private state read, API-key read, raw-payload read, materialization, T0/cache/outcome/backtest/model execution, profitability evaluation, or human-gate consumption; git fetch/push to origin were the only network operations. `V9_014_DESIGN_HIGH_1=REMEDIATION_IMPLEMENTED_AWAITING_GPT_REVIEW` and `V9_014_DESIGN_HIGH_2=REMEDIATION_IMPLEMENTED_AWAITING_GPT_REVIEW`. Current stage is `V9_014_DESIGN_REMEDIATION_AWAITING_GPT_REVIEW`; next action is `GPT_EXACT_SHA_V9_014_DESIGN_REMEDIATION_REVIEW`.

## 2026-09-01 — V9_014 design MEDIUM_1 share-unit token mapping freeze

- GPT-5.6 Sol exact-SHA review of `48fb8c59975b5afa967c6ff34e3e9343a8551c51` (parent `05d0ed3ca5cea8e84ff386fb73c65dba575030c2`) is recorded as `BLOCK` with `CRITICAL=0`, `HIGH=0`, `MEDIUM=1`. `V9_014_DESIGN_HIGH_1=RESOLVED` and `V9_014_DESIGN_HIGH_2=RESOLVED`. `MEDIUM_1=DECLARED_SHARE_UNIT_TOKEN_TO_MULTIPLIER_MAPPING_NOT_EXACTLY_FROZEN`. This task remediates MEDIUM_1 only; no V9_014 design PASS is claimed.
- The accepted Trading Volume share-unit semantics are now frozen exactly. There are exactly two canonical semantic units: `SHARES` with `multiplier=1` and `THOUSAND_SHARES` with `multiplier=1000`. Parser recognition must use exact normalized semantic tokens taken from the declared unit cell, never fuzzy substring matching, because the source PDF may present the declared unit bilingually.
- Accepted English tokens are frozen as `"shs." -> SHARES` and `"thous.shs." -> THOUSAND_SHARES`. Accepted Japanese equivalents are frozen as `"株" -> SHARES` and `"千株" -> THOUSAND_SHARES`, recognized only when they occur in the same declared bilingual unit cell. A bilingual declaration must be semantically consistent — `株` with `shs.` is valid `SHARES`, and `千株` with `thous.shs.` is valid `THOUSAND_SHARES` — and any contradictory bilingual declaration is a data-quality failure.
- Never accepted: guessed abbreviations, fuzzy matches, case-insensitive invented aliases, multipliers inferred from numeric magnitude, value-based unit guessing, unit inheritance from a previous month, or any other multiplier. Any other unit token or value is `UNSUPPORTED_SHARE_UNIT_DQ_FAILURE`. The exact interval rule is unchanged and restated over the two frozen units with integer arithmetic only: `SHARES` yields `[q,q]` and `THOUSAND_SHARES` yields `[q*1000, q*1000+999]`.
- The required unit expectations are now explicitly frozen per required segment and column. For `ERA_PRE` through `2022-04-01`: 1st Section, 2nd Section, JASDAQ Standard, and JASDAQ Growth are `THOUSAND_SHARES` for both total and ToSTNeT; Mothers total is `THOUSAND_SHARES`, and Mothers ToSTNeT is `SHARES` for `2017-01..2019-12` and `THOUSAND_SHARES` for `2020-01..2022-04-01`. For `ERA_POST` from `2022-04-04`: Prime, Standard, and Growth are `THOUSAND_SHARES` for both total and ToSTNeT. The observed declared unit must match the exact expected unit, and any mismatch is a data-quality failure.
- The previous generic rule permitting non-Mothers required columns to establish their unit by observed intra-era constancy is deleted and replaced. There is no first-observed-unit learning: a required column's unit is never established by observed constancy, inferred from the data, re-derived from numeric magnitude, or inherited from a previous month or object. The preregistered expectation is the only authority, and the parsed declaration is checked against it rather than defining it.
- All other frozen V9_014 design semantics are preserved unchanged: the interval proof rule, `NOT_PROVEN` semantics, the SOURCE_A preserved-chain binding, the SOURCE_B roles, SOURCE_C, the required segments, the 2022-04 two-part bundle, `EXPECTED_UNPROVEN_SET`, the cross-source consistency requirement, the prohibitions on retry/refetch/post-hoc correction, and `OPEN_1` through `OPEN_5` all `CLOSED`. No `CHATGPT_DECISION_REQUIRED` item remains in this design.
- `V9_012_RESULT=FAIL_TERMINAL`, `V9_013_RESULT=DIAGNOSTIC_COMPLETE`, `V9_013_DIAGNOSTIC_CLASS=RELATION_OR_SENTINEL_FAILURE`, `V9_009_HIGH_2=OPEN_REQUIRES_HISTORICAL_JPX_CALENDAR_BINDING`, `V9_009_MEDIUM_1=OPEN`, `T0_STATUS=NOT_RUN`, and `future_profitability_established=false` all remain unchanged. This design-remediation-only task made no implementation, code, or test change, and performed no Python execution, JPX/J-Quants/Yahoo/external-data acquisition, protected or private state read, API-key read, raw-payload read, materialization, T0/cache/outcome/backtest/model execution, profitability evaluation, or human-gate consumption; git fetch/push to origin were the only network operations. `V9_014_DESIGN_MEDIUM_1=REMEDIATION_IMPLEMENTED_AWAITING_GPT_REVIEW`. Current stage is `V9_014_DESIGN_MEDIUM_1_AWAITING_GPT_REVIEW`; next action is `GPT_EXACT_SHA_V9_014_DESIGN_MEDIUM_1_REVIEW`.

## 2026-09-01 — V9_014 implementation: core synthetic contract

- Implements only the deterministic OFFLINE core and synthetic contract frozen by the V9_014 design at git SHA `efee3d0efca368645c00aeed63cb8e0637cd3672` (design blob SHA `2bbacbf37ab961d1cbf416b7fd476db18778c5b7`, verified to match `git rev-parse HEAD:V9_014_JPX_MONTHLY_AUCTION_ACTIVITY_AUTHORITY_SUCCESSOR_DESIGN_DRAFT.md` before writing any code). No GPT independent review of this implementation has occurred yet; this is an executor submission only.
- New module `src/v9_014_jpx_monthly_auction_activity_authority.py` freezes exact provenance constants (coverage, 109 logical months, the 110 required physical SOURCE_B objects, SOURCE_A/B/C roles and identities, the reused preserved V9_012 SOURCE_A chain SHA-256 and page count with `SOURCE_A_FRESH_ACQUISITION_AUTHORIZED=False`), the PRE/POST required segments with TOKYO PRO Market excluded, the 2022-04 two-part object-bundle rule, `EXPECTED_UNPROVEN_SET`, and the two neighbor sentinels.
- `resolve_declared_unit` implements the exact frozen share-unit token mapping: only the four literal tokens `"shs."`, `"thous.shs."`, `"株"`, `"千株"` are recognized by exact string equality; a Japanese token is accepted only paired with its matching English token in the same cell; contradictory or ambiguous (multiply-declared) token sets fail closed; any other token is `UNSUPPORTED_SHARE_UNIT_DQ_FAILURE`. No fuzzy matching, case-folding, invented alias, magnitude inference, or previous-month inheritance exists anywhere in the module.
- `share_interval` implements the exact frozen interval rule (`m=1` -> `[q,q]`, `m>1` -> `[q*m, q*m+(m-1)]`) using only Python `int` arithmetic; no `float` appears anywhere in the module. `classify_segment` implements the exact frozen per-segment rule (`DQ` iff `total_upper < tostnet_lower`; `DEFINITELY_AUCTION_ACTIVE` iff `total_lower > tostnet_upper`; else `NOT_PROVEN`), the explicit dash-total-with-positive-ToSTNeT structural-impossibility DQ case, and the invariant that a dash can never by itself prove activity. There is no status representing proven inactivity anywhere in the module; SOURCE_B can prove activity only.
- `classify_date` evaluates only the frozen required segments for a date's era in frozen order, fails closed on any missing required segment/table (covering OPEN_5's missing-coverage rule) and on any declared-unit mismatch against the exact frozen per-segment/column/era expectation table (including the Mothers ToSTNeT split at `2020-01-01`), and never reads or requires TOKYO PRO Market. `validate_source_b_object_bundle` implements the 2022-04 two-part bundle rule and rejects the prohibited single-object claim for April 2022, plus duplicate/unexpected-part and unknown-month failures.
- `source_c_confirmed_exception_set` mirrors the frozen SOURCE_C requirement that both assertions (no Auction Market execution; ToSTNeT orders by 08:56 had executions) must be present before the fixed, non-parameterized document date `2020-10-01` is confirmed; the date is a module constant and is never caller-injectable. `evaluate_cross_source_relation` implements the exact frozen relation, the two independent neighbor-sentinel checks, and the cross-source consistency requirement, operating only on caller-supplied synthetic evidence. ## 2026-09-01 — V9_014 implementation MEDIUM_1: SOURCE_B coverage closure

- GPT-5.6 Sol exact-SHA review of `b08db8dfc06598a05fd069d9e4222754cdaf3b87` is recorded as `BLOCK` with `CRITICAL=0`, `HIGH=0`, `MEDIUM=1`. `MEDIUM_1=SOURCE_B_COVERAGE_CLOSURE_NOT_MECHANICALLY_ENFORCED`. This task remediates MEDIUM_1 only; no GPT PASS, no overall V9_014 implementation PASS, no V9_009 HIGH_2 resolution, and no exact frozen validation PASS is claimed by this executor submission.
- The finding: `evaluate_cross_source_relation` previously accepted a bare, caller-asserted `proven_auction_active_dates` list with no proof that SOURCE_B date/table coverage was ever actually processed for every scheduled-open date. A date entirely missing from that list -- whether legitimately `NOT_PROVEN` or never evaluated at all because its required SOURCE_B table was absent -- was indistinguishable, so a relation could reach `RELATION_PASS` purely because the resulting left-set difference happened to equal `EXPECTED_UNPROVEN_SET`, even when SOURCE_B never actually covered that date.
- Item 1 remediation (collection-level SOURCE_B object completeness): new `validate_source_b_object_collection(entries)` in `src/v9_014_jpx_monthly_auction_activity_authority.py` takes a sequence of `(logical_month, present_parts)` pairs (not a mapping, so a duplicate logical-month entry is representable and detectable) and mechanically proves the complete caller-supplied collection covers exactly the 109 frozen logical months `2017-01..2026-01`, each exactly once, each with exactly its frozen required object parts via the existing `validate_source_b_object_bundle` (the `2022-04` two-part bundle included), and exactly `110` total required physical parts. It reuses the existing frozen object-part identities and month-sequence constants; it invents no alternative object identity, URL, archive numbering, or locator methodology.
- Item 2 remediation (date/table coverage closure): `evaluate_cross_source_relation`'s signature is changed from a bare `proven_auction_active_dates: Sequence[str]` parameter to `source_b_date_classifications: Mapping[str, DateClassification]` -- the actual per-date result produced by `classify_date`. The function now computes `missing_coverage_dates` (scheduled-open dates absent from the mapping entirely) and `dq_coverage_dates` (scheduled-open dates present but classified `DQ`) and requires both sets to be empty (`coverage_complete=True`) before `RELATION_PASS` is reachable; `proven_auction_active_dates` is now derived internally from classifications whose status is exactly `PROVEN_AUCTION_ACTIVE`. A date that exists and is genuinely classified `NOT_PROVEN` (case A) remains legitimately permitted and distinct from a date whose required coverage is entirely absent (case B); the two no longer collapse to the same observable state. Extra SOURCE_B coverage for a date outside `scheduled_open_dates` still surfaces via a non-empty `right_diff` and fails closed, so it cannot silently broaden authority. The `RelationEvaluation` result gained `coverage_complete`, `missing_coverage_dates`, and `dq_coverage_dates` fields for exactly this purpose; no `trading_dates` materialization was added.
- All previously frozen semantics are unchanged: the `m=1`/`m>1` interval rule, integer-only arithmetic, the segment `DQ`/`DEFINITELY_AUCTION_ACTIVE`/`NOT_PROVEN` rule and its dash/blank/malformed/unit-mismatch cases, the date-level "at least one segment definitely active" rule, SOURCE_B's inability to prove inactivity, the exact unit-token set with no fuzzy/case-folded/invented aliases, the Mothers ToSTNeT unit split, the PRE/POST required segments with TOKYO PRO Market excluded, `EXPECTED_UNPROVEN_SET={"2020-10-01"}`, the two neighbor sentinels, the SOURCE_C exact exception relation, and the prohibition on materializing `trading_dates`.

- GPT-5.6 Sol exact-SHA review of `a91218cfe79ca5fc7fc420ce7611bbe100e61d6e` is recorded as `BLOCK` with `CRITICAL=0`, `HIGH=0`, `MEDIUM=1`. `MEDIUM_1=SOURCE_B_COVERAGE_CLOSURE_REMEDIATION_INCOMPLETE_OBJECT_COLLECTION_PASS_NOT_REQUIRED_BY_RELATION`. This task closes the remaining mechanical part of the same MEDIUM_1; no GPT PASS, no overall V9_014 implementation PASS, no V9_009 HIGH_2 resolution, and no exact frozen validation PASS is claimed by this executor submission.
- The finding: the round-1 remediation correctly validated the complete SOURCE_B object collection and correctly distinguished present-and-`NOT_PROVEN` date coverage from missing/`DQ` coverage, but `evaluate_cross_source_relation` never required the object-collection validation result, so a caller could supply an invalid or incomplete physical-object collection alongside a perfect `DateClassification` mapping and still reach `RELATION_PASS`. Separately, the relation only explicitly rejected `status == DQ`; an unknown, misspelled, synthetic, or invented `DateClassification.status` was not excluded and could in principle behave like a covered date.
- Remediation item 1: `evaluate_cross_source_relation` in `src/v9_014_jpx_monthly_auction_activity_authority.py` gained a required fourth parameter, `object_collection: ObjectCollectionValidation` -- the actual result produced by the existing `validate_source_b_object_collection`, reusing its frozen constants and methodology with no new collection logic invented. `RELATION_PASS` is now unreachable unless `object_collection.status == SOURCE_B_OBJECT_COLLECTION_OK`; this is exposed as the new `object_collection_valid: bool` field on `RelationEvaluation`. Any collection failure -- missing month, unexpected month, duplicate month, a missing `2022-04` PRE or normal object, a duplicate/unexpected part, or a physical-count mismatch -- now makes `RELATION_PASS` unreachable regardless of how perfect the per-date evidence is.
- Remediation item 2: a new closed constant `VALID_DATE_CLASSIFICATION_STATUSES = frozenset({PROVEN_AUCTION_ACTIVE, NOT_PROVEN, DQ})` is introduced, and the relation now computes `invalid_status_dates` -- scheduled-open dates whose classification status falls outside that closed set -- folding it into `coverage_complete` alongside the existing `missing_coverage_dates`/`dq_coverage_dates` checks. An unknown status is therefore excluded from `proven_active` (it can never equal `PROVEN_AUCTION_ACTIVE`) and additionally fails `coverage_complete` explicitly, so it can never silently behave like a legitimate `NOT_PROVEN` date. No `PROVEN_INACTIVE` or equivalent state exists anywhere in the module.

- GPT-5.6 Sol exact-SHA review of `396d3271991641d6753927772690c44f0fd404c0` is recorded as `BLOCK` with `CRITICAL=0`, `HIGH=1`, `MEDIUM=0`. `V9_014_SOURCE_B_LOCATOR_HIGH_1=SELECTED_YEAR_CHILD_SNAPSHOT_PROVENANCE_UNBOUND`. A candidate is eligible only when its own `parent_year_page_url` equals that exact bound URL, in addition to its existing label/month match; a parent mismatch folds into the existing `LOCATOR_ZERO_CANDIDATES_FAILURE` outcome even when the candidate's report label, month, JPX domain, and PDF suffix are all otherwise valid. The same binding pattern is applied identically to the April-2022 PRE special-reference branch, per the review's explicit instruction to bind it the same way.
- All previously frozen locator semantics are unchanged: exact label/month matching with no fuzzy/case-folded/substring aliasing, the 109-logical-month/110-physical-request planner and its 2022-04 split, PDF-only enforcement on both final-child paths, single-candidate uniqueness, and the guarantee that a resolved URL always originates from a supplied href. `resolve_source_b_year_page` and its `RootYearCandidate` input are unchanged -- this finding and its remediation are scoped to the year-page-to-child binding only, not the root-to-year step.
- Targeted synthetic tests in `tests/test_v9_014_jpx_monthly_auction_activity_source_b_locator.py` grew from 34 to 43, all synthetic with no real JPX HTML/PDF and no network/filesystem access. New coverage proves: root selects year X, child snapshot parent X passes (using the real `resolve_source_b_year_page` output as the bound parent); root selects year X, a normal-month child snapshot parent Y fails; the same for the April-PRE branch; a matching-label candidate with a mismatched parent cannot bypass the binding even alongside another correctly-parented candidate for a different month; a structurally valid JPX PDF from an unrelated parent cannot bypass the binding; an absent/malformed `selected_year_page_url` fails closed as invalid input for both paths; an off-domain `selected_year_page_url` fails closed as an invalid-URL failure; and parenthood is proven not inferred from a child href containing a plausible year substring that matches the requested year while its declared `parent_year_page_url` points elsewhere. ## 2026-09-01 — V9_014 SOURCE_B archive-HTML HIGH_1 PASS recorded; extracted-row/object binding added

- GPT-5.6 Sol exact-SHA review of `f39e7eafbb826c38c910c506404d8077342e4ddc` is recorded as `PASS` with `CRITICAL=0`, `HIGH=0`, `MEDIUM=0`, `LOW=0`. `V9_014_SOURCE_B_ARCHIVE_HTML_HIGH_1=RESOLVED` and `V9_014_IMPLEMENTATION_SOURCE_B_OFFLINE_ARCHIVE_HTML_CANDIDATE_EXTRACTION=PASS`. - This task implements a new, separate deterministic OFFLINE module, `src/v9_014_jpx_monthly_auction_activity_source_b_row_binding.py`, that binds caller-supplied, already-extracted SYNTHETIC SOURCE_B daily rows to their frozen physical-object identity (a logical month plus one of its frozen required object parts) and, only after binding succeeds, classifies every bound row through the existing, unchanged core `classify_date`. The core module, locator module, archive-parser module, and their tests are all unmodified.
- Input model: `SourceBDailyRow(date: str, segment_cells: Mapping[str, Mapping[str, Cell]])` reuses the exact `Mapping` shape and the existing core `Cell` types (`BlankCell`/`DashCell`/`MalformedCell`/`NumericCell`) unchanged -- `NumericCell.unit_tokens` are already-normalized exact semantic tokens; this task performs no raw unit-cell text normalization. `SourceBObjectRowBundle(logical_month, object_part, rows)` carries the explicit claimed physical-object identity, reusing `REQUIRED_LOGICAL_MONTHS`, `APRIL_2022_LOGICAL_MONTH`, `PRE_APRIL_1_REFERENCE_OBJECT`, `NORMAL_MONTHLY_REPORT2_OBJECT`, `required_source_b_object_parts`, `era_for_date`, and `classify_date` verbatim from the core module.
- `bind_source_b_object_rows` enforces, in deterministic first-failure order: (1) `logical_month` must be one of the frozen 109 months and `object_part` must be exactly one of `required_source_b_object_parts(logical_month)`; (2) each row date must be a strict `YYYY-MM-DD` string that round-trips through `date.fromisoformat().isoformat()`, checked in row order, with a bool/non-string/malformed value failing closed; (3) no two rows in one bundle may share a date; (4) for `NORMAL_MONTHLY_REPORT2_OBJECT` the row collection must be non-empty, every row's calendar month must equal `logical_month` exactly, and every row must fall in a known frozen era -- for `logical_month == "2022-04"` specifically, every row must fall in `ERA_POST` (2022-04-04 onward), so `2022-04-01` and the two non-business gap days are all rejected in the normal object; (5) for `PRE_APRIL_1_REFERENCE_OBJECT` every supplied row must have date exactly `2022-04-01` (reused from the core's own frozen `ERA_PRE_END` rather than a duplicated literal) and that date must actually be present. Only after every check passes is `classify_date` called once per row, in row order, and its result -- including any `DQ` -- returned completely unmodified in an insertion-ordered `date_classifications` mapping; on any binding failure that mapping is `None`, making a successful, classification-bearing result structurally unreachable. The closed status vocabulary (`OBJECT_ROW_BINDING_OK` plus nine named failure constants) introduces no `PROVEN_INACTIVE` or other new scientific classification.
- New synthetic test file `tests/test_v9_014_jpx_monthly_auction_activity_source_b_row_binding.py` (22 tests, all synthetic, reusing the exact `_pre_era_segment_cells`/`_post_era_segment_cells` fixture pattern already established in the core test suite, no real JPX/PDF data and no network/filesystem access) covers: normal PRE- and POST-era happy paths whose classifications are asserted identical to calling `core.classify_date` directly; the Mothers ToSTNeT pre-2020 `SHARES`/2020-onward `THOUSAND_SHARES` semantics proven to remain entirely core-controlled (including an unexpected-unit DQ case identical to a direct `classify_date` call); a missing required segment and a blank/malformed cell both proven to yield the exact same core `DQ` result unchanged; duplicate dates, dates outside the logical month, six varieties of malformed/non-canonical dates, and a `bool` date all failing object binding with `date_classifications=None`; an empty normal-object collection failing; an invalid object-part/month combination and an unrecognized object-part string both failing before any row is considered; an invalid logical month failing; the April-2022 PRE object accepting exactly `2022-04-01` and rejecting its absence or any other date (including `2022-04-04`, `2022-03-31`, and `2021-04-01`); the April-2022 normal object rejecting `2022-04-01` and both non-business gap days while accepting a `2022-04-04` `ERA_POST` row; a source-text scan confirming no `float` conversion or arithmetic exists in the module; and confirmation the module carries no `trading_dates`, relation, or profitability surface.

- GPT-5.6 Sol exact-SHA review of `c515bcf36e642b1435dec0c3c94df0db566d2568` is recorded as `BLOCK` with `CRITICAL=0`, `HIGH=0`, `MEDIUM=1`, `LOW=0`. `MEDIUM_2=E14_NO_NETWORK_REMOTE_HEAD_CURRENTNESS_NOT_MECHANICALLY_VERIFIABLE`.
